pratyakṣaṃ lokataḥ siddhaṃ lokāś cāgamapūrvakāḥ
śiṣṭācāro bahuvidho brūhi tan me pitāmaha
bhīṣma uvāca
tatas tair bhidyate vṛttaṃ śṛṇu caiva yudhiṣṭhira
avṛttyā ye ca bhindanti śrutatyāgaparāyaṇāḥ
dharmavidveṣiṇo mandā ity uktas teṣu saṃśayaḥ
atṛpyantas tu sādhūnāṃ ya evāgamabuddhayaḥ
param ity eva saṃtuṣṭās tān upāssva ca pṛccha ca
kāmārthau pṛṣṭhataḥ kṛtvā lobhamohānusāriṇau
na teṣāṃ bhidyate vṛttaṃ yajñasvādhyāyakarmabhiḥ
ācāraḥ kāraṇaṃ caiva dharmaś caiva trayaṃ punaḥ
yudhiṣṭhira uvāca
apāre mārgamāṇasya paraṃ tīram apaśyataḥ
vedāḥ pratyakṣam ācāraḥ pramāṇaṃ tat trayaṃ yadi
bhīṣma uvāca
yady evaṃ manyase rājaṃs tridhā dharmavicāraṇā
ukto mārgas trayāṇāṃ ca tat tathaiva samācara
jijñāsā tu na kartavyā dharmasya paritarkaṇāt
sadaiva bharataśreṣṭha mā te bhūd atra saṃśayaḥ
andho jaḍa ivāśaṅko yad bravīmi tad ācara
brāhmaṇeṣu ca vṛttir yā pitṛpaitāmahocitā
tām anvehi mahābāho svargasyaite hi deśikāḥ
pramāṇam apramāṇaṃ vai yaḥ kuryād abudho naraḥ
eteṣv eva tv ime lokāḥ kṛtsnā iti nibodha tān
yudhiṣṭhira uvāca
bravītu bhagavān etat kva te gacchanti tādṛśāḥ
bhīṣma uvāca
dharma eva ratis teṣām ācāryopāsanād bhavet
manuṣyā yadi vā devāḥ śarīram upatāpya vai
prathamaṃ brahmaṇaḥ putraṃ dharmam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ
dharmiṇaḥ paryupāsante phalaṃ pakvam ivāśayaḥ
yudhiṣṭhira uvāca
asatāṃ kīdṛśaṃ rūpaṃ sādhavaḥ kiṃ ca kurvate
bravītu me bhavān etat santo 'santaś ca kīdṛśāḥ
bhīṣma uvāca
durācārāś ca durdharṣā durmukhāś cāpy asādhavaḥ
sādhavaḥ śīlasaṃpannāḥ śiṣṭācārasya lakṣaṇam
rājamārge gavāṃ madhye goṣṭhamadhye ca dharmiṇaḥ
nopasevanti rājendra sargaṃ mūtrapurīṣayoḥ
pañcānām aśanaṃ dattvā śeṣam aśnanti sādhavaḥ
na jalpanti ca bhuñjānā na nidrānty ārdrapāṇayaḥ
citrabhānum anaḍvāhaṃ devaṃ goṣṭhaṃ catuṣpatham
brāhmaṇaṃ dhārmikaṃ caityaṃ te kurvanti pradakṣiṇam
vṛddhānāṃ bhārataptānāṃ strīṇāṃ bālāturasya ca
brāhmaṇānāṃ gavāṃ rājñāṃ panthānaṃ dadate ca te
atithīnāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ preṣyāṇāṃ svajanasya ca
tathā śaraṇakāmānāṃ goptā syāt svāgatapradaḥ
sāyaṃ prātar manuṣyāṇām aśanaṃ devanirmitam
nāntarā bhojanaṃ dṛṣṭam upavāsavidhir hi saḥ
homakāle yathā vahniḥ kālam eva pratīkṣate
ṛtukāle tathā nārī ṛtum eva pratīkṣate
amṛtaṃ brāhmaṇā gāva ity etat trayam ekataḥ
yajuṣā saṃskṛtaṃ māṃsam upabhuñjan na duṣyati
karma vai saphalaṃ kṛtvā gurūṇāṃ pratipādayet
gurūn abhyarcya vardhante āyuṣā yaśasā śriyā
vṛddhān nātivadej jātu na ca saṃpreṣayed api
nāsīnaḥ syāt sthiteṣv evam āyur asya na riṣyate
na nagnām īkṣate nārīṃ na vidvān puruṣān api
maithunaṃ satataṃ guptam āhāraṃ ca samācaret
tīrthānāṃ guravas tīrthaṃ śucīnāṃ hṛdayaṃ śuci
sāyaṃ prātaś ca vṛddhānāṃ śṛṇuyāt puṣkalā giraḥ
śrutam āpnoti hi naraḥ satataṃ vṛddhasevayā
svādhyāye bhojane caiva dakṣiṇaṃ pāṇim uddharet
yacched vāṅmanasī nityam indriyāṇāṃ ca vibhramam
saṃskṛtaṃ pāyasaṃ nityaṃ yavāgūṃ kṛsaraṃ haviḥ
aṣṭakāḥ pitṛdaivatyā vṛddhānām abhipūjanam
śmaśrukarmaṇi maṅgalyaṃ kṣutānām abhinandanam
vyādhitānāṃ ca sarveṣām āyuṣaḥ pratinandanam
avarāṇāṃ samānānāṃ śiṣyāṇāṃ ca samācaret
pāpam ācakṣate nityaṃ hṛdayaṃ pāpakarmiṇām
jñānapūrvaṃ vinaśyanti gūhamānā mahājane
jñānapūrvaṃ kṛtaṃ karma cchādayante hy asādhavaḥ
na māṃ manuṣyāḥ paśyanti na māṃ paśyanti devatāḥ
pāpenābhihataḥ pāpaḥ pāpam evābhijāyate
yathā vārdhuṣiko vṛddhiṃ dehabhede pratīkṣate
prāyaścittahataṃ pāpaṃ tathā sadyaḥ praṇaśyati
tasmāt pāpaṃ na gūheta gūhamānaṃ vivardhate
kṛtvā tu sādhuṣv ākhyeyaṃ te tat praśamayanty uta
āśayā saṃcitaṃ dravyaṃ yat kāle nopabhujyate
mānasaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ dharmam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ
nidhiṃ nidadhyāt pāratryaṃ yātrārthaṃ dānaśabditam
yudhiṣṭhira uvāca
nābhāgadheyaḥ prāpnoti dhanaṃ subalavān api
bhāgadheyānvitas tv arthān kṛśo bālaś ca vindati
kṛtayatnāphalāś caiva dṛśyante śataśo narāḥ
nālabhyaṃ copalabhyeta nṛṇāṃ bharatasattama
yadā prayatnaṃ kṛtavān dṛśyate hy aphalo naraḥ
mārgan nayaśatair arthān amārgaṃś cāparaḥ sukhī
akāryam asakṛt kṛtvā dṛśyante hy adhanā narāḥ
dhanayuktās tv adharmasthā dṛśyante cāpare janāḥ
adhītya nītiṃ yasmāc ca nītiyukto na dṛśyate
anabhijñaś ca sācivyaṃ gamitaḥ kena hetunā
yadi vidyām upāśritya naraḥ sukham avāpnuyāt
na vidvān vidyayā hīnaṃ vṛttyartham upasaṃśrayet
yathā pipāsāṃ jayati puruṣaḥ prāpya vai jalam
dṛṣṭārtho vidyayāpy evam avidyāṃ prajahen naraḥ
bhīṣma uvāca
īhamānaḥ samārambhān yadi nāsādayed dhanam
dānena bhogī bhavati medhāvī vṛddhasevayā
ahiṃsayā ca dīrghāyur iti prāhur manīṣiṇaḥ
tasmād dadyān na yāceta pūjayed dhārmikān api
svābhāṣī priyakṛc chuddhaḥ sarvasattvāvihiṃsakaḥ
yadā pramāṇaprabhavaḥ svabhāvaś ca sukhāsukhe
maśakīṭapipīlānāṃ sthiro bhava yudhiṣṭhira
bhīṣma uvāca
tatrāśvasīta satkṛtvā asatkṛtvā na viśvaset
buddhim āviśya bhūtānāṃ dharmārtheṣu pravartate
tadāśvasīta dharmātmādṛḍhabuddhir na viśvaset
etāvan mātram etad dhi bhūtānāṃ prājñalakṣaṇam
yathā hy upasthitaiśvaryāḥ pūjayante narā narān
evam evātmanātmānaṃ pūjayantīha dhārmikāḥ
tasmād viśuddham ātmānaṃ jānīyād dharmacāriṇam
yudhiṣṭhira uvāca
vipāpmā ca bhavet kena kiṃ vā kalmaṣanāśanam
bhīṣma uvāca
ayaṃ daivatavaṃśo vai ṛṣivaṃśasamanvitaḥ
dvisaṃdhyaṃ paṭhitaḥ putra kalmaṣāpaharaḥ paraḥ
pitāmaho jagannāthaḥ sāvitrī brahmaṇaḥ satī
umāpatir virūpākṣaḥ skandaḥ senāpatis tathā
śakraḥ śacīpatir devo yamo dhūmorṇayā saha
varuṇaḥ saha gauryā ca saha ṛddhyā dhaneśvaraḥ
saumyā gauḥ surabhir devī viśravāś ca mahān ṛṣiḥ
ṣaṭkālaḥ sāgaro gaṅgā sravantyo 'tha marudgaṇāḥ
vālakhilyās tapaḥsiddhāḥ kṛṣṇadvaipāyanas tathā
tumbaruś citrasenaś ca devadūtaś ca viśrutaḥ
dharmaḥ satyaṃ tapo dīkṣā vyavasāyaḥ pitāmahaḥ
śarvaryo divasāś caiva mārīcaḥ kaśyapas tathā
śukro bṛhaspatir bhaumo budho rāhuḥ śanaiścaraḥ
vainateyāḥ samudrāś ca kadrujāḥ pannagās tathā
gaṅgā mahānadī caiva kapilā narmadā tathā
kampunā ca viśalyā ca karatoyāmbuvāhinī
sarayūr gaṇḍakī caiva lohityaś ca mahānadaḥ
tāmrāruṇā vetravatī parṇāśā gautamī tathā
godāvarī ca veṇṇā ca kṛṣṇaveṇā tathādrijā
dṛṣadvatī ca kāverī vaṃkṣur mandākinī tathā
puṇyatīrthaiś ca kalilaṃ kurukṣetraṃ prakīrtitam
sindhūttamaṃ tapodānaṃ jambūmārgam athāpi ca
hiraṇvatī vitastā ca tathaivekṣumatī nadī
bhūmibhāgās tathā puṇyā gaṅgādvāram athāpi ca
ṛṣikulyās tathā medhyā nadī citrapathā tathā
mahendravāṇī tridivā nīlikā ca sarasvatī
nandā cāparanandā ca tathā tīrthaṃ mahāhradam
gayātha phalgutīrthaṃ ca dharmāraṇyaṃ surair vṛtam
vindhyo dhātuvicitrāṅgas tīrthavān auṣadhānvitaḥ
śṛṅgavān mandaro nīlo niṣadho darduras tathā
citrakūṭo 'ñjanābhaś ca parvato gandhamādanaḥ
puṇyaḥ somagiriś caiva tathaivānye mahīdharāḥ
diśaś ca vidiśaś caiva kṣitiḥ sarve mahīruhāḥ
viśvedevā nabhaś caiva nakṣatrāṇi grahās tathā
pāntu vaḥ satataṃ devāḥ kīrtitākīrtitā mayā
devatānantaraṃ viprāṃs tapaḥsiddhāṃs tapodhikān
kīrtitān kīrtayiṣyāmi sarvapāpapramocanān
bhadrāṃ diśaṃ mahābhāgā ulmucuḥ pramucus tathā
mitrāvaruṇayoḥ putras tathāgastyaḥ pratāpavān
dṛḍhāyuś cordhvabāhuś ca viśrutāv ṛṣisattamau
paścimāṃ diśam āśritya ya edhante nibodha tān
uṣadguḥ saha sodaryaiḥ parivyādhaś ca vīryavān
atreḥ putraś ca dharmātmā tathā sārasvataḥ prabhuḥ
uttarāṃ diśam āśritya ya edhante nibodha tān
atrir vasiṣṭhaḥ śaktiś ca pārāśaryaś ca vīryavān
ṛcīkapautro rāmaś ca ṛṣir auddālakis tathā
lomaśo nāciketaś ca lomaharṣaṇa eva ca
ādyaḥ prakīrtito rājan sarvapāpapramocanaḥ
nṛgo yayātir nahuṣo yaduḥ pūruś ca vīryavān
dhundhumāro dilīpaś ca sagaraś ca pratāpavān
kṛśāśvo yauvanāśvaś ca citrāśvaḥ satyavāṃs tathā
duḥṣanto bharataś caiva cakravartī mahāyaśāḥ
yavano janakaś caiva tathā dṛḍharatho nṛpaḥ
rāmo rākṣasahā vīraḥ śaśabindur bhagīrathaḥ
karaṃdhamo naraśreṣṭhaḥ kadhmoraś ca narādhipaḥ
dakṣo 'mbarīṣaḥ kukuro ravataś ca mahāyaśāḥ
mucukundaś ca rājarṣir mitrabhānuḥ priyaṃkaraḥ
mahābhiṣaś ca vikhyāto nimirājas tathāṣṭakaḥ
āyuḥ kṣupaś ca rājarṣiḥ kakṣeyuś ca narādhipaḥ
śibir auśīnaraś caiva gayaś caiva narādhipaḥ
pratardano divodāsaḥ saudāsaḥ kosaleśvaraḥ
ailo nalaś ca rājarṣir manuś caiva prajāpatiḥ
havidhraś ca pṛṣadhraś ca pratīpaḥ śaṃtanus tathā
kakṣasenaś ca rājarṣir ye cānye nānukīrtitāḥ
vaiśaṃpāyana uvāca
tūṣṇīṃbhūte tadā bhīṣme paṭe citram ivārpitam
nṛpaṃ śayānaṃ gāṅgeyam idam āha vacas tadā
rājan prakṛtim āpannaḥ kururājo yudhiṣṭhiraḥ
sahito bhrātṛbhiḥ sarvaiḥ pārthivaiś cānuyāyibhiḥ
upāste tvāṃ naravyāghra saha kṛṣṇena dhīmatā
yudhiṣṭhiraṃ sahāmātyam anujajñe nadīsutaḥ
uvāca cainaṃ madhuraṃ tataḥ śāṃtanavo nṛpaḥ
yayātir iva rājendra śraddhādamapuraḥsaraḥ
rañjayasva prajāḥ sarvāḥ prakṛtīḥ parisāntvaya
anu tvāṃ tāta jīvantu mitrāṇi suhṛdas tathā
caityasthāne sthitaṃ vṛkṣaṃ phalavantam iva dvijāḥ
dhṛtarāṣṭraṃ puraskṛtya gāndhārīṃ ca pativratām
saha tair ṛṣibhiḥ sarvair bhrātṛbhiḥ keśavena ca
praviveśa kuruśreṣṭha puraṃ vāraṇasāhvayam
vaiśaṃpāyana uvāca
tataḥ kuntīsuto rājā paurajānapadaṃ janam
pūjayitvā yathānyāyam anujajñe gṛhān prati
sāntvayām āsa nārīś ca hatavīrā hateśvarāḥ
vipulair arthadānaiś ca tadā pāṇḍusuto nṛpaḥ
so 'bhiṣikto mahāprājñaḥ prāpya rājyaṃ yudhiṣṭhiraḥ
avasthāpya naraśreṣṭhaḥ sarvāḥ svaprakṛtīs tadā
pratigṛhyāśiṣo mukhyās tadā dharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ
uṣitvā śarvarīḥ śrīmān pañcāśan nagarottame
dṛṣṭvā nivṛttam ādityaṃ pravṛttaṃ cottarāyaṇam
ghṛtaṃ mālyaṃ ca gandhāṃś ca kṣaumāṇi ca yudhiṣṭhiraḥ
prasthāpya pūrvaṃ kaunteyo bhīṣmasaṃsādhanāya vai
dhṛtarāṣṭraṃ puraskṛtya gāndhārīṃ ca yaśasvinīm
mātaraṃ ca pṛthāṃ dhīmān bhrātṝṃś ca puruṣarṣabhaḥ
mahatā rājabhogyena paribarheṇa saṃvṛtaḥ
stūyamāno mahārāja bhīṣmasyāgnīn anuvrajan
niścakrāma purāt tasmād yathā devapatis tathā
āsasāda kurukṣetre tataḥ śāṃtanavaṃ nṛpam
upāsyamānaṃ vyāsena pārāśaryeṇa dhīmatā
hataśiṣṭair nṛpaiś cānyair nānādeśasamāgataiḥ
rakṣibhiś ca mahātmānaṃ rakṣyamāṇaṃ samantataḥ
śayānaṃ vīraśayane dadarśa nṛpatis tataḥ
tato rathād avārohad bhrātṛbhiḥ saha dharmarāṭ
dvaipāyanādīn viprāṃś ca taiś ca pratyabhinanditaḥ
ṛtvigbhir brahmakalpaiś ca bhrātṛbhiś ca sahācyutaḥ
āsādya śaratalpastham ṛṣibhiḥ parivāritam
bhrātṛbhiḥ saha kauravya śayānaṃ nimnagāsutam
yudhiṣṭhiro 'haṃ nṛpate namas te jāhnavīsuta
śṛṇoṣi cen mahābāho brūhi kiṃ karavāṇi te
ācāryā brāhmaṇāś caiva ṛtvijo bhrātaraś ca me
putraś ca te mahātejā dhṛtarāṣṭro janeśvaraḥ
upasthitaḥ sahāmātyo vāsudevaś ca vīryavān
hataśiṣṭāś ca rājānaḥ sarve ca kurujāṅgalāḥ
tān paśya kuruśārdūla samunmīlaya locane
evam uktas tu gāṅgeyaḥ kuntīputreṇa dhīmatā
dadarśa bhāratān sarvān sthitān saṃparivārya tam
diṣṭyā prāpto 'si kaunteya sahāmātyo yudhiṣṭhira
parivṛtto hi bhagavān sahasrāṃśur divākaraḥ
aṣṭapañcāśataṃ rātryaḥ śayānasyādya me gatāḥ
śareṣu niśitāgreṣu yathā varṣaśataṃ tathā
māgho 'yaṃ samanuprāpto māsaḥ puṇyo yudhiṣṭhira
tribhāgaśeṣaḥ pakṣo 'yaṃ śuklo bhavitum arhati
dhṛtarāṣṭram athāmantrya kāle vacanam abravīt
rājan viditadharmo 'si sunirṇītārthasaṃśayaḥ
bahuśrutā hi te viprā bahavaḥ paryupāsitāḥ
vedaśāstrāṇi sarvāṇi dharmāṃś ca manujeśvara
vedāṃś ca caturaḥ sāṅgān nikhilenāvabudhyase
śrutaṃ devarahasyaṃ te kṛṣṇadvaipāyanād api
yathā pāṇḍoḥ sutā rājaṃs tathaiva tava dharmataḥ
tān pālaya sthito dharme guruśuśrūṣaṇe ratān
dharmarājo hi śuddhātmā nideśe sthāsyate tava
ānṛśaṃsyaparaṃ hy enaṃ jānāmi guruvatsalam
tava putrā durātmānaḥ krodhalobhaparāyaṇāḥ
īrṣyābhibhūtā durvṛttās tān na śocitum arhasi
vaiśaṃpāyana uvāca
vāsudevaṃ mahābāhum abhyabhāṣata kauravaḥ
anujānīhi māṃ kṛṣṇa vaikuṇṭha puruṣottama
rakṣyāś ca te pāṇḍaveyā bhavān hy eṣāṃ parāyaṇam
vāsudevena tīrthena putra saṃśāmya pāṇḍavaiḥ
saṃdhānasya paraḥ kālas taveti ca punaḥ punaḥ
na ca me tad vaco mūḍhaḥ kṛtavān sa sumandadhīḥ
ghātayitveha pṛthivīṃ tataḥ sa nidhanaṃ gataḥ
tvāṃ ca jānāmy ahaṃ vīra purāṇam ṛṣisattamam
nareṇa sahitaṃ devaṃ badaryāṃ suciroṣitam
tathā me nāradaḥ prāha vyāsaś ca sumahātapāḥ
naranārāyaṇāv etau saṃbhūtau manujeṣv iti
vāsudeva uvāca
anujānāmi bhīṣma tvāṃ vasūn āpnuhi pārthiva
na te 'sti vṛjinaṃ kiṃ cin mayā dṛṣṭaṃ mahādyute
pitṛbhakto 'si rājarṣe mārkaṇḍeya ivāparaḥ
vaiśaṃpāyana uvāca
evam uktas tu gāṅgeyaḥ pāṇḍavān idam abravīt
dhṛtarāṣṭramukhāṃś cāpi sarvān sasuhṛdas tathā
prāṇān utsraṣṭum icchāmi tan mānujñātum arhatha
ānṛśaṃsyaparair bhāvyaṃ sadaiva niyatātmabhiḥ
brahmaṇyair dharmaśīlaiś ca taponītyaiś ca bhārata
punar evābravīd dhīmān yudhiṣṭhiram idaṃ vacaḥ
brāhmaṇāś caiva te nityaṃ prājñāś caiva viśeṣataḥ
ācāryā ṛtvijaś caiva pūjanīyā narādhipa
vaiśaṃpāyana uvāca
evam uktvā kurūn sarvān bhīṣmaḥ śāṃtanavas tadā
tūṣṇīṃ babhūva kauravyaḥ sa muhūrtam ariṃdama
tasyordhvam agaman prāṇāḥ saṃniruddhā mahātmanaḥ
idam āścaryam āsīc ca madhye teṣāṃ mahātmanām
yad yan muñcati gātrāṇāṃ sa śaṃtanusutas tadā
kṣaṇena prekṣatāṃ teṣāṃ viśalyaḥ so 'bhavat tadā
saṃniruddhas tu tenātmā sarveṣv āyataneṣu vai
maholkeva ca bhīṣmasya mūrdhadeśāj janādhipa
niḥsṛtyākāśam āviśya kṣaṇenāntaradhīyata
evaṃ sa nṛpaśārdūla nṛpaḥ śāṃtanavas tadā
yudhiṣṭhiras tu gāṅgeyaṃ viduraś ca mahāmatiḥ
uṣṇīṣe paryagṛhṇītāṃ mādrīputrāv ubhau tadā
tālavṛntāny upādāya paryavījan samantataḥ
yājakā juhuvuś cāgniṃ jaguḥ sāmāni sāmagāḥ
tataś candanakāṣṭhaiś ca tathā kāleyakair api
samavacchādya gāṅgeyaṃ prajvālya ca hutāśanam
apasavyam akurvanta dhṛtarāṣṭramukhā nṛpāḥ
saṃskṛtya ca kuruśreṣṭhaṃ gāṅgeyaṃ kurusattamāḥ
jagmur bhāgīrathītīram ṛṣijuṣṭaṃ kurūdvahāḥ
kṛṣṇena bharatastrībhir ye ca paurāḥ samāgatāḥ
vidhivat kṣatriyaśreṣṭhāḥ sa ca sarvo janas tadā
utthāya salilāt tasmād rudatī śokalālasā
rājavṛttena saṃpannaḥ prajñayābhijanena ca
satkartā kuruvṛddhānāṃ pitṛbhakto dṛḍhavrataḥ
jāmadagnyena rāmeṇa purā yo na parājitaḥ
divyair astrair mahāvīryaḥ sa hato 'dya śikhaṇḍinā
sametaṃ pārthivaṃ kṣatraṃ kāśipuryāṃ svayaṃvare
hataṃ śikhaṇḍinā śrutvā yan na dīryati me manaḥ
pīḍito nātiyatnena nihataḥ sa śikhaṇḍinā
evaṃvidhaṃ bahu tadā vilapantīṃ mahānadīm
āśvāsayām āsa tadā sāmnā dāmodaro vibhuḥ
samāśvasihi bhadre tvaṃ mā śucaḥ śubhadarśane
gataḥ sa paramāṃ siddhiṃ tava putro na saṃśayaḥ
vasur eṣa mahātejāḥ śāpadoṣeṇa śobhane
sa eṣa kṣatradharmeṇa yudhyamāno raṇājire
dhanaṃjayena nihato naiṣa nunnaḥ śikhaṇḍinā
bhīṣmaṃ hi kuruśārdūlam udyateṣuṃ mahāraṇe
na śaktāḥ syur nihantuṃ hi raṇe taṃ sarvadevatāḥ
ity uktā sā tu kṛṣṇena vyāsena ca saridvarā
tyaktvā śokaṃ mahārāja svaṃ vāry avatatāra ha
satkṛtya te tāṃ saritaṃ tataḥ kṛṣṇamukhā nṛpāḥ
anujñātās tayā sarve nyavartanta janādhipāḥ
